## `AUS-0128-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AUS` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Curing Technician
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Medicinal Plant Active-Part Harvest & Curing
- **Intro:** Curing Technician — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Medicinal Plant Active-Part Harvest & Curing — Pre-Shift Cold Check and Chamber Entry: Chamber temperature must be between 0°C and 4°C. If temperature > 4°C, do not enter.
